HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001376/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_04b2da62fe8998cd3788","text":"2. **A spectrum from covert attack to exposed confrontation.** Fixed `ل م ز B001` specifically includes eye, mouth, and hidden-speech signaling, whereas fixed `ه م ز B003` supplies overt prodding and fixed `ه م ز B004` supplies public disparagement. Later `104:4` activates `ن ب ذ B002`, open repudiation or confrontation. The sequence is supplied, but I infer the escalation: concealed signaling licenses increasingly exposed force until the aggressor is himself openly repudiated. The paired agent-nouns therefore retain distinct roles rather than collapsing into synonyms: one covertly marks a defect, while the other drives the marked person through repeated pressure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001376/B001","root_001376/B002","root_001600/B001","root_001600/B003","root_001600/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_217af8e197c687a29f7a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:1:3\",\"root_001600\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"104:1:4\",\"root_001376\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Karalama ve ayıplama, yalnızca söz değil, insanları sıkıştıran, dürten ve yerinden eden bedensel bir kuvvet gibi işler; sonraki ezilme bu kuvveti sahibine döndürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001315/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_276c2bba293fe287ee1c","text":"4. **Dulling beneath sharp contempt.** Evidence: `ك ل ل B001` (loss of sharpness in tongue, eye, hearing, sight), activated by `ح س ب B002` in 104:3 and `ف ء د B002` in 104:7. Structural cue: the speech-crime is followed by faulty reckoning and then exposure of the heart. Assigned role: كلال supplies inward dulling beneath outward verbal sharpness. Abductive move: a person can be socially sharp because inward perception is morally dulled. Reading change: the aggressor is not simply keen-tongued; his tongue and sight are compromised by a deeper dullness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001600/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_33da4fa278b8a54656ea","text":"2. **Open vocal attack plus hidden signaling.** Evidence: `ه م ز B002` (pressing a letter in speech), `ه م ز B004`, and `ل م ز B001` (eye, mouth, or hidden speech). Structural cue: the paired nouns keep two modes side by side, then 104:5-7 move from knowing to fire that reaches hearts. Assigned role: همزة marks audible articulation; لمزة marks covert insinuation. Abductive move: because the later fire exposes inner seats, the initial vice includes hidden micro-signals as well as public insult. Reading change: 104:1 reads as a social technology of harm—voice, gesture, and concealed intent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001376/B001","root_001600/B002","root_001600/B003","root_001600/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3db526664b051751fa7f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:1:3\",\"root_001600\",[\"B002\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]],[\"104:1:4\",\"root_001376\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İki niteleme, açık ve sesli saldırı ile bakış, ağız hareketi ya da örtülü sözle yapılan gizli saldırıyı birlikte kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001315/B001","root_001315/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_466ef34ed484c8aabdc9","text":"3. **Exploratory edge-reversal inside `لِّكُلِّ`.** The fixed quantifier is directly `ك ل ل B003` (totality), encompassing each habitual attacker; the dormant `ك ل ل B001` image of a blade or tongue losing sharpness is activated by the two speech-agents and by the later breaker `ح ط م B001`. Grammar supplies universal scope, while the transfer from the dulling branch to the agents is my abductive move. The line can consequently carry an irony: those who perform social sharpness are comprehensively placed under a process that removes edge and breaks the instrument. This remains exploratory because `كُلِّ` itself straightforwardly realizes totality, not dullness.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6637d0b1717c0e090cf1","text":"104:2-3 showed that the speech-type is coupled to an accounting self, so the contempt is not free-floating gossip but speech generated from stored wealth and false حساب. 104:7 then made the hidden part decisive: the fire reaches the أفئدة, so the لمز branch of covert signaling becomes central rather than secondary.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001315/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6fd62a8e91ca9e0a599f","text":"3. **\"Every\" as an enclosing totality.** Evidence: `ك ل ل B003` (totality/completeness), activated by `و ص د B001` and `ع م د B003` in 104:8-9. Structural cue: كل begins as a quantifier and the surah ends in comprehensive enclosure. Assigned role: كل gathers the type without exception. Abductive move: the totalizing judgment at the beginning anticipates total closure at the end. Reading change: \"for every\" becomes more than distribution; it previews an all-around consequence fitted to an all-encompassing habit.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B001","root_001600/B003","root_001600/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7af7e92281dc8094fa22","text":"1. **Speech functioning as applied force.** Fixed `هُمَزَةٍ` has `ه م ز B004` (taunting and backbiting), but `ه م ز B001` (squeezing by hand) and `B003` (prodding or impelling) become active beside fixed `لُّمَزَةٍ`, whose `ل م ز B001` is covert fault-finding and whose `B002` is pushing or striking. `104:4` then supplies casting (`ن ب ذ B001`) into the breaker (`ح ط م B001`). The packet supplies a repeated lexical bridge from social injury to bodily pressure and then material destruction; I infer that verbal disparagement transfers force to its target. The two nouns thus read as operators in a pressure mechanism, not as harmless speech-types.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001376/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a19b898d23fb38465ed4","text":"`104:4` made the physical branches of both fixed roots harder to dismiss: casting into `ح ط م B001` completes the movement already latent in squeezing, prodding, pushing, and striking. `104:6` then added sustained kindling rather than a single impact. The surprise was that the pair maps not merely two speech habits but a scalable force-process; the covert specificity of `ل م ز B001` still prevents the two nouns from being flattened into one sense.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001315/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b8e18f0c333ca599b5e7","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:1:2\",\"root_001315\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"“Her” sözüyle kurulan eksiksiz kapsam, surenin sonunda saldırganların her yanını kuşatan kapanmayı önceden sezdirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001466/B001","root_001600/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e4d522c74087eb86a419","text":"1. **Speech as pressure that returns as crushing.** Evidence: `ه م ز B004` (taunting/backbiting) and `ل م ز B001` (covert fault-finding), with physical undertones from `ه م ز B001/B003` (pressing, prodding) and `ل م ز B002` (pushing/striking). Structural cue: the speech-pair at the start is answered by `ن ب ذ B001` and `ح ط م B001` in 104:4. Assigned role: the two speech roots supply the initial social force. Abductive move: verbal contempt is treated as pressure applied to others, so later crushing is a material return of that force. Reading change: the ayah condemns not merely insulting content but an embodied practice of squeezing, prodding, and displacing people through speech.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001315/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_fa6400df4cadf77ba4a0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:1:2\",\"root_001315\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Başkalarına karşı keskin görünen dilin altında, görüşü ve kavrayışı körelmiş bir iç durum bulunduğu ima edilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
